                                  Preface.

       "Velir-Varalaru" is a learned and a laborious production of Mr. M.
       Raghava Aiyangar of Ramnad. The sterling worth of the work has been
       attested to by the Royal Asiatic Society. The Madras University has
       set upon it its own seal of approval. In the face of these
       distinguished testimonials, any prefatory remarks of mine own,
       which I have been called upon by the learned author to advance, are
       not at all needed.
       Till quite recent times the study of Tamil literature was confined
       solely to the aroma and the ornate side of it. Of late Tamil
       literature is being studied with a view to build up its history.
       The author here tries to pursue the pure history side imbedded in
       the regions of ancient Tamil works. He here gives the early history
       of the Velir probably the original ancestors of the community known
       at present by the name of Vellalas. His copious references to
       various authorities lend considerable support to this position.
       According to the author the Velir originally belong to the Yadhava
       clan - the very clan in which Sri Krishna was born. Sometime after
       the Mahabharatha war, the Velir, it seems migrated from Guzerat,
       their original abode, to South India, leaving their outstanding
       traces wherever they lived. They can be gathered from the names of
       towns in that part of India beginning with Vel or its corrupted
       forms, Belhutti, Bela, Belgaum, Velapur. The author concludes that
       the Bellalas, Chalukyas, Andhras, Yadhavas and Velir come from the
       same stock of people and that many of the
       Velir held sway over portions of South India either as paramount
       rulers or
       as cheifs in subordinate capacity. As the author sketches it, the
       history of a few Velir, is an informing and an interesting reading.
       I have attempted in these few lines but a perfunctory resume of the
       work - a real research work.
       The Thesis evidences a breadth of consultation and a compression of
       materials. The way in which the author cogently premises to suggest
       the inferency reflects a rare critical insight and a fine sense of
       discrimination. The materials, which the author has marshalled,
       subserve an investigation on kindred and collateral topics, I
       fervently hope that Mr. M. Raghava Aiyangar and scholar of his type
       will render similar service to the country.
       S.RM. M. RAMASAMY CHETTIAR,
       Chidambaram.
       ---------

                                nūṉmukam.

       cila varuṣaṅkaṭkumuṉ caṅkacceyyuḷkaḷai yāṉ ārāycci ceytuvantapōtu,
       avaṟṟiṟ kaṇṭa ciṟṟaracarkaḷilē vēḷir eṉṟu kuṟikkappaṭṭavar oru
       taṉiyāṉa kulattavar eṉpataṟkuc cilapala cāṉṟukaḷ kiṭaittaṉa. ataṉāl
       akkūṭṭattāraip paṟṟip piṉpu cevvaiyāka ārāyumpaṭi nērntatōṭu,
       avvārāycciyiṉ payaṉāka vēḷirkulattaippaṟṟiya muṭivāṉa koḷkaikaḷ
       cilavum ēṟpaṭṭaṉa. avaṟṟai aṟiñar cilarkku aṟivittukkoṇṭatil,
       aṉṉōrum eṉ koḷkaikaḷai āmōtittu ūkkiṉar. piṉpu, 1905-m varuṣam
       nikaḻnta maturait tamiḻc caṅkattiṉ nāṉkām varuṣakkūṭṭattē, " The
       Tamils 1800-years ago" eṉṟa ariya tamiḻccaritanūliṉ āciriyarāka
       viḷaṅkiya srīmān- vi. kaṉakacapaippiḷḷai avarkaḷatu akkirācaṉattiṉ
       kīḻk kūṭiya vitvatsamūkattiṉ muṉpu, eṉṉārāyccikaḷait tiraṭṭiyeḻuti
       araṅkēṟṟalāyiṉēṉ. avviyācam, accaṅkattuc centamiḻp pattirikaiyilum
       piṉpu veḷiyiṭappeṟṟatu. pattirikaiyil veḷivanta ataṉaippaṭitta nam
       nāṭṭaṟiñar palar, eṉ koḷkaikaḷiṟ ṟamakkuḷḷa cammatattait tāmē
       makiḻcciyuṭaṉ eṉakku aṟivittārkaḷ. aṉṉōr apippirāyaṅkaḷ cila
       aṭuttup patippikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ivarkaḷuḷ, koḻumpu srīmān-
       vi.je.tampippiḷḷaiyavarkaḷ,M.R.A.S., tammuṭam pāṭaṟivittatu
       maṭṭumaṉṟi, āṅkilattil ivviyācattai aḻakuṟa moḻi peyarttup pala
       kīḻkkuṟippukaḷum iṭṭu--ilaṅkaip pakuti rāyal ēṣiyāṭik sosaiṭikku
       aṉuppi ataṉai ---------
       page iv
       veḷiyiṭuvittārkaḷ.* ivarkaḷāl aṉpukūrntu ceyyappeṟṟa peru naṉṟi
       eṉṉāl eṉṟum maṟakkattakkataṉṟu.
       iṅṅaṉam, nallaṟiñar pallōratu naṉmatippu icciṟu viyācattukku
       ēṟpaṭṭatu eṉakkup piṉṉum ūkkamaḷittamaiyāl vēḷirkulattil aṟivāṟṟal
       perumai mutaliya uyarkuṇaṅkaḷ vāyntu tamiḻakampukaḻa viḷaṅkiya
       paḻaiya vaḷḷalkaḷ cilaratu varalāṟukaḷaiyum centamiḻil muṟaiyē
       eḻuti veḷiyiṭalāṉēṉ.
       ippōtu, ivvēḷir varalāṟṟaik kaṉantaṅkiya ceṉṉaic carvakalā
       caṅkattārum (University) naṉku matittu, tamiḻ kaṟkum māṇavarkku
       upayōkamāmpaṭi, 1914-m varuṣattu "iṇṭer-mīṭiyaṭ" ( Intermediate)
       parīkṣā pāṭaṅkaḷuḷ oṉṟāka niyamittiruttalaṟintu makiḻkiṉṟēṉ.
       ikkauravamaḷittataṉ poruṭṭu caṅkattārkku mikavum naṉṟiceluttuṅ
       kaṭappāṭuṭaiyēṉ.
       vēḷir viṣayamāka eḻutappeṟṟa eṉ viyācaṅkaḷai oruṅku tiraṭṭi
       veḷiyiṭuvataṟku itaṉaiyē takka camayamākakkoṇṭu, ௸ ārāyccikaḷai
       maṟumuṟai cōtittup pala tiruttaṅkaḷuṭaṉum kīḻkkuṟippu
       anupantaṅkaḷuṭaṉum oru ciṟu nūlāka ippōtu veḷiyiṭṭirukkiṟēṉ. itaṉuṭ
       kaṇṭa vēḷiruḷḷē pāri eṉṟa vaḷḷaliṉ varalāṟu muḻutum, eṉ arumpeṟaṉ
       maittuṉarum sētu samstāṉa vittuvāṉumākiya srī: rā. irākavaiyaṅkār
       avarkaḷāṟ cevvitiṉ eḻutappeṟṟuc centamiḻil muṉṉarē veḷivantuḷḷatu.
       avvārāycciyurai innūṟkup peritum ēṟṟatāṉamaiyāl, aiyaṅkāravarkaḷatu
       aṉpuṭaiyaṉumati peṟṟu, ataṉai ivviyācattukku ēṟṟapeṟṟiyil amaittu
       veḷiyiṭṭirukkiṟēṉ.

       * Journal of the Royal Asiatic Society Ceylon-Branch No.61-1908

       innūl centamiḻp pattirikai vāyilāka muṉpu veḷivantatākaliṉ,

       veḷiyiṭappeṟṟatu.
       ivviyācattē, paḻaiya vēḷiruṭaiya varalāṟukaḷai ārāycci ceytatiṉ
       palaṉāka ēṟpaṭṭa koḷkaikaḷaikkūṟutalē eṉ nōkkamaṉṟi, cātipaṟṟiya
       evvaḻakkilum piravēcippatu eṉ karuttaṉṟu eṉpatai aṟiñarkkellām

       page vi

        vēḷir varalāṟṟaippaṟṟiya cila apippirāyaṅkaḷ

       (1) mahāmahōpātyāyar: prammasrī. vē. cāmināta aiyar avarkaḷ;-- "
       inta muṟai centamiḻil veḷippaṭṭa naṉṉaṉ--vēṇmāḷ mutaliya niyāsaṅkaḷ
       mikka āṉantattai uṇṭupaṇṇiṉa. mikavum kaṭiṉamākiya caṅkacceyyuḷiṟ
       pukuntu ārāyntu oḻuṅkāka viṣayaṅkaḷai eṭutteḻutuvataik kāṭṭilum
       ciṟanta arumai vēṟu yātuḷatu? ellām avviṭattup peru muyaṟciyāl
       vanta payaṉē"--( srīmān-- pāṇṭitturait tēvaravarkaṭku 3--5--06-l
       eḻutiya kaṭitappakuti)
       (2) ";centamiḻ" ppattirāciriyarāyirunta-sētusamstāṉa vitvāṉ srī rā.
       irākavaiyaṅkār avarkaḷ:-- nī centamiḻil eḻutivarum viyācaṅkaḷ
       peritum matikkattakkaṉavāyuḷḷaṉa. " āyaṇṭiraṉ, naṉṉaṉ vēṇmāṉ"
       mutaliya vēḷirvaralāṟukaḷaip paṭittupperumakiḻ vaṭaintēṉ. uṉṉāl
       eḻutappaṭṭuḷḷa viyācaṅkaḷellām, nuṇṇiya ārāycciyoṭukūṭiyē
       viḷaṅkutalāl, iṉi eḻutappaṭuvaṉavum avvāṟu viḷaṅkat taṭaiyillai
       eṉṟē nampukiṟēṉ. (5--3--06)
       (3) srīmat- tirumaṇam- celvakkēcavarāya mutaliyār avarkaḷ M.A.,
       paccaiyappaṉ kālēj, ceṉṉai;---- aiyarē! puṟanāṉūṟu vakuppiṟ
       pāṭamāka naṭakkumpōtu atil iruṅkōvēḷiṉ ātikka muraikkum iṭattilum
       nacciṉārkkiṉiyar, vēḷiriṉ ātikka muraikkiṉṟa iṭattilum piṟṟai nāḷ
       varalāṟukaḷuṭaṉ toṭarpupaṭutta iyalātu iṭarppaṭṭuviṭṭēṉ. āyiṉum,
       tāṅkaḷ eḻutiya vēḷir varalāṟṟil iṉṟiyamaiyāta carittirak kūṟukaḷum
       mēṟkōḷkaḷum paṟṟācāṉa cāṉṟukaḷum--" poṉṉum tukirum muttum
       maṉṉiya--māmalai payanta kāmaru maṇiyum-iṭaipaṭaccēya vāyiṉun toṭai
       -----------
       page vii
       puṇarntu-aruvilai naṉkalam amaikkuṅ kālai--oruvaḻit tōṉṟi
       yāṅku"--iyaipupaṭat toṭarpuṭaiyaṉavāy amaintiruttal kaṇṭu aṭaṅkā
       makiḻcci aṭaintuḷēṉ. taṅkaḷ viyācattaip paṭittavuṭaṉē, toṇṭai
       maṇṭalattu uyartuḷuva vēḷāḷar eṉṉum eṅkaḷ pūrvōttaram ippoḻutu
       veḷḷiṭai vilaṅkalāka viḷakkameytukiṉṟatu. taṅkaḷ viyācattaip
       paṭitta aḷavilē eṉpāleḻunta makiḻcci, yāṉ uḷḷapaṭi celvak
       kēcavarāyaṉāyiṉ, uṭaṉē varicaikaḷpala ceyyumāṟu eṉṉai ēviyirukkum;
       yāṉ cētupatiyallaṉē, pāṇṭitturaiccāmiyallaṉē, putukkōṭṭait
       toṇṭaimāṉ allaṉē...........itaṉāl, eṉṉuḷaṅkoḷā makiḻcci eṉṉaiviṭṭu
       eṉṉuḷḷattai īrttukkoṇṭu taṅkaḷpāṟ ceṉṟuviṭṭatu. ataṉ makiḻcciyai
       aṅkīkarittu ataṉai eṉpāl viṭukka. (1905-jūlai.5)
       (4) prammasrī: S. irātākiruṣṇaiyar avarkaḷ, piriṉsipāl, makārājā
       kālēj, putukkōṭṭai:-- taṅkaḷāl eḻutappeṟṟa paḻaiya kaittoḻil
       viyāpāraṅkaḷ, vēḷir varalāṟu, mātait tiruvēṅkaṭanātar, poyyāmoḻip
       pulavar, vīrattāymār mutaliyavai vantu cērntaṉa. ivaṟṟuṭ cilavaṟṟai
       muṉṉarē vācittirukkiṟēṉ. ivai mikka ārāycciyaik kāṇpikkiṉṟaṉa eṉṟu
       yāvarum oppukkoḷvārkaḷ. tāṅkaḷ tamiḻukkeṉṟu uḻaittu varuvatupōl
       anēkar uḻaittāl, maṟaintu kiṭakkum pala ariya viṣayaṅkaḷ eḷitil
       veḷivarum eṉpatil cantēkamillai. ( 5--2--1908.)
       (5) srīmat-J.M. nallacāmip piḷḷaiyavarkaḷ B.A., B.L., ṭi.muṉsīp,
       rājamahēntiram--(17-4-1910) "puṟapporuḷ veṇpāmālai puṟanāṉūṟu
       ivaikaḷiṉiṉṟum, ṭākṭar ji.yū. pōppaiyaravarkaḷāl moḻi
       peyarkkappaṭṭavaikaḷai ippoḻutu acciṭṭuvarukiṉṟēṉ. nāṉ eḻuti varum
       ataṉ uraippāyirattiṟkut tāṅkaḷ eḻutiyuḷḷa "vāṉmīkarum teṉṉāṭum"
       eṉṟa viṣayaṅkaḷum mikavum mukkiyamāṉavai. vēḷāṇ māntaraip paṟṟiya
       eṉatu cantēkam, itupoḻutu kaṇṭa vēḷir varalāṟṟiṉāl oḻintatākum.
       ivvārāycciyil tāṅkaḷ ceytuḷḷatu ciṟappikkaṟ pālatu.
       -------
       page viii
       taṅkaḷ pattirikai yovvoṉṟum āṅkilattil moḻi peyarkkappaṭiṉ
       nammavarkkum airōppiyarkkum peritum pirayōcaṉappaṭum; eṉakkuc ceyya
       mikavum avāvuṇṭu; kālamiṉmaiyē taṭai. tāṅkaḷ eḻutiyuḷḷa
       viṣayaṅkaḷai yellām eṉṉuraiyil pēciyirukkiṟēṉ."
       (6) misṭar ṭi. cavarirāyap piḷḷaiyavarkaḷ, M.R.A.S., talaimait
       tamiḻp paṇṭitar, ceṉṭ-jōsaps-kālēj. tirucci:-- " tāṅkaḷ
       centamiḻppattirikaiyiṟ pattirātipa-āsaṉattiṉ kīḻ eḻutivaruvaṉa
       ārāyccit tiṟam amaintavai: mika arumaiyāṉa viṣayaṅkaḷ. taṅkaḷ
       ārāyccit tiṟattaiyum matinuṭpattaiyum ekkālattum peritum
       viyakkiṉṟēṉ.................. tāṅkaḷ veḷiyiṭṭa vēḷir varalāṟṟaik
       koḻumpu srīmān-V.J. tampip piḷḷaiyavarkaḷ M.R.A.S., āṅkilattil
       moḻipeyarttu "rāyal-ēṣiyāṭik-sosaiṭi" kku aṉuppiyirukkiṟārkaḷ.
       avviṣayam accaṅkattārāl oppukkoḷḷappaṭṭatu. iccaṅkattiṉ 'memparṣip'
       tāṉ 'M.R.A.S.,-- eṉpatu, taṅkaḷukkum ikkauravam urittāka
       vēṇṭumeṉpatu eṉ atyanta viruppam.......... iṉṉum tāṅkaḷ eḻutiyuḷḷa
       paḻaiya kaittoḻil viyāpāraṅkaḷ, vīrattāymār mutaliya viṣayaṅkaḷ
       mika arumaiyāṉavai: tamiḻariṉ paṇṭaipperumaikaḷai naṉku viḷakkuvaṉa
       (7--11--7)
       (7) Brahma Sri. K..Sundara Rama Aiyer Avergal, M.A., Professor of
       History, Kumbakonam College," I read 2- articles in "Sen-Tamil"
       with very great interest, vēḷir varalāṟu and pāratakālamum
       tamiḻarum. I was taken by surprise at the erudition displayed and
       the ingenuity of the suggestions and inference made regarding the
       past history of S. India. The writer expresses himself also with
       unusual reform of style that-- a style that commends itself to me
       atleast. It is that which has most affinity to the vigour and ease
       of modern English Writers. I trust Mr. mu. irākavaiyaṅkār will
       continue his researches and writings and convey more instruction
       regarding ancient S. India.
       ---------------
       page ix
       I regard his writings altogether as of an epoch-making character
       and trust he will keep the promise he has given of a future of
       renown. It is now that the work of our great scholar Mr. Aiyer
       Avergal, is bearing fruit" (August 26th 05.)
       (8) Sri., V.Gopala Aiyer, B.A., B.L, (Author of Chronology of
       Ancient India, Chittoor):-- " I am much obliged to you for your
       instructive essay on Velir, a copy of which was duly received by me
       a few days ago. The various lines of reasoning by which you seek to
       prove your theories are the evident result of your varied and
       extensive study of Tamil history and literature and I hope you will
       continue your interesting researches. I am glad to find you support
       the date I have given for the great Mahabharata War." (11--7--05.)
       (9) Sri., V.J. Thambi Pillai Avergal, M.R., A.S. Audit office,
       Colombo.--" May I take the liberty to write to you that I intend to
       have an English Translation of your very interesting paper entitled
       vēḷir varalāṟu appearing in the last but one issue of 'Sen-Tamil"
       published in the journal of the Royal Asiatic Society here. It is
       full of facts that ought to be more generally known. I had arrived
       at the same conclusions nearly two years ago. I hope that S.India,
       will yet reveal facts of history that will try to unite all the
       great races of India into one great nation. I shall send you a copy
       when printed" (August11,05'),
       ii (12th, June.07.) " Dear Sir, You will be very glad to hear that
       the committee of the Royal Asiatic Society, Ceylon Branch have
       informed me to-day that they have accepted with thanks my
       -------------
       page x
       English tanslation of your interesting paper on the history of the
       Vels [ vēḷirvaralāṟu.] Your paper is no doubt, one of the great
       merit and deserves to be published in the Journal of the Royal
       Asiatic Society for the perusal of learned men all over the world.
       You are indebted, I think, to me, for bringing your idea and
       theories to such prominence. I have done it as a labour of love.
       Your paper on 'Tamil Alphabet' is most interesting and original in
       some respects."

       page 1
       srī:

                            vēḷir varalāṟu

       centamiḻ vaḷartta perumaiyiṟ cēra cōḻa pāṇṭiyarkku evvaḷavu
       pākamuṇṭō, ataṟku atikamillāmaṟpōyiṉum, avaraḷavu camapākam
       peṟṟuniṉṟa oru peruṅ kūṭṭattār muṟkālattē tamiḻnāṭṭil
       viḷaṅkiyiruntaṉar eṉiṉ, atu cilarkku viyappākat tōṟṟalām. āṉāl,
       paḻaiya nūlārāycciyuṭaiya centamiḻaṟiñarkku atu ciṟitum viyappaṉṟu.
       paṇṭai yilakkiyaṅkaḷai ārāyumiṭattu, mūvēntar paṭaitta
       tamiḻpperumaiyaḷavu tāmum paṭaittu niṉṟa oru kūṭṭattār tamiḻakattē
       viḷakkamuṟṟirunta ceyti veḷiyākum. iṉṉōr tām vēḷir*
       eṉṟaḻaikkap-paṭuvārkaḷ. iravāmalīnta kaṭaiyeḻu vaḷḷalkaḷiṟ† palar
       ivarkaḷē. “koṭukkilā tāṉaip pāriyē yeṉṟu kūṟiṉuṅ koṭuppārilai”‡
       eṉṉum tiruvākkil, vaḷḷaṉmaikkē ōrellaiyāka vaittup pārāṭṭap
       peṟṟavaṉ ivaruḷ oruvaṉēyāvaṉ. varaiyāvaṇmaiyāl tāṉaṭainta oppaṟṟa
       pukaḻaikkaṇṭu mūvēntarum$ aḻukkāṟu koḷḷumpaṭi niṉṟa ipperuvaḷḷal
       viḷaṅkiyatu vēḷir kulameṉiṉ, ataṟku vēṟoru perumaiyum vēṇṭumō?

   * vēḷir, vēḷ eṉpataṉ paṉmaiccol: kēḷ kēḷir eṉṟāṟpōla.
       † pāri, ōri, malaiyaṉ, eḻiṉi, pēkaṉ, āy, naḷḷi eṉṟa eḻuvar.
       (puṟam-158; ciṟupāṇ - aṭi. 84--122.)
       ‡ srī: cuntaramūrttināyaṉār tēvāram.
       $ ivaṉ vēḷ - pāriyāvaṉ; ivaṉatu vaḷḷaṉmaiyāl uṇṭākiya perumpukaḻ,
       tamiḻaracarākiya cēra cōḻa pāṇṭiyaratukīrttikaḷaiyuṅkīḻppaṭuttu
       mēmpaṭṭataṉāl aḻukkāṟukoṇṭa avvaracarkaḷ, tammiṟkūṭi avvēḷiṉ
       paṟampumalaiyai muṟṟukaiyiṭṭu, avvaḷḷalaiyum vañcattāṟ koṉṟaṉar.
       iccaritavirivai, innūluḷ vēḷ-pāri eṉṟa talaippiṉkīḻk kaṇṭukoḷka.

   page 2
   iṅṅaṉam pukaḻvāyntakulattavariṉ varaḷāṟupaṟṟi nammavarāṟ ciṟappāka
   ārāyntaṟiyappaṭṭavai ituvarai illaiyeṉṟē collalām. centamiḻaracar eṉṟa
   apimāṉattāṟ cēra cōḻa pāṇṭiyaratu varalāṟukaḷai aṟivatiṟṟamiḻ makkaḷāl
   ettuṇai ūkkaṅ kāṭṭappaṭukiṉṟatō, ataṉiṟ ciṟu pakutiyēṉum ivvēḷir
   viṣayattiṉuṅ kāṭṭutal nammavaratu peruṅ kaṭamaiyaṉṟō. āṉāl,
   immuyaṟciyil iṟaṅkattakka karuvikaḷ kiṭaippa tariteṉṟu collakkūṭum.
   vēḷiratu paramparai varalāṟukaḷai eḻutivaitta paḻaiya nūlkaḷēṉum
   cātaṉaṅkaḷēṉum kiṭaiyāveṉpatu uṇmaiyē. ippōtu kāṇappaṭuvatellām,
   puṟanāṉūṟu mutaliya cilapaḻaiya tamiḻ nūlkaḷiṟ pulavarkaḷ avvappōtu
   pāṭippōnta oru cila ceyyuṭkaḷēyām. ic cilavaṟṟaikkoṇṭu vēḷir
   varalāṟellān terital eṅṅaṉaṅ kūṭum? eṉiṉum, vēḷir varalāṟākiya iruṇṭa
   kaḷañciyattē, kaiyiluḷḷa icciṟu veḷiccaṅkaḷait tuṇaik koṇṭu
   kūṭiyavaḷavu nām turuvuvōm. aṅṅaṉan turuvi nōkkuvatiṟ ṟerikiṉṟavaṟṟait
   tamiḻmakkaṭku veḷiyiṭa muyalvōm.
   paḻaiya caṅkanūlkaḷilē, vēḷir eṉṟa oru kūṭṭattār, tamiḻp
   pēraracarkaḷākiya cēra cōḻa pāṇṭiyarai aṭuttup pala viṭaṅkaḷiṉuṅ
   kūṟappaṭukiṉṟaṉar; itaṉai:--“paṇkeḻu vēntarum vēḷirum” eṉap
   puṟanāṉūṟṟiṉum, “iruperu vēntaroṭu vēḷir” eṉa maturaikkāñciyiṉum,
   “vēntarum vēḷirum” eṉap patiṟṟuppattiṟ palaviṭaṅkaḷiṉum varutalāl
   aṟiyalām. itaṉāl, mūvēntaraiyumaṭuttu muṟkālattē tamiḻnāṭṭil
   matikkappaṭṭa ciṟṟaracaruḷ, ivvēḷirē muṟpaṭṭavar eṉpatu viḷaṅkum.
   aṉṟiyum “toṉmutir vēḷir” (akam - 258; puṟam - 24) “nāṟpattoṉpatu
   vaḻimuṟaivanta vēḷir” (puṟam - 201) “iruṅkōvēḷ maruṅku” (paṭṭiṉappālai)
   “evvitolkuṭi” (puṟam - 202) eṉa nūlkaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭutaliṉ, iṉṉōr
   paṇṭaikkāla mutalē tamiḻnāṭṭil viḷaṅkiyiruntavar eṉpatum
   teḷiyappaṭukiṉṟatu.
   iṉi, ivvēḷir yāvar? ivar kulam yātu? ivar tamiḻnāṭṭiṉ paḻaiya makkaḷā?
   aṉṟi iṭaiyil vantēṟiyavarā? ivar

   * 'patiṉeṇkōṭi' eṉa accuppatippil uḷḷatāyiṉum, ēṭṭuppiratikaḷil

   kaṇṇapirāṉ vaḻiyiṉarākiya aracaraiyum vēḷiraiyum teṉṉāṭṭiṟ
   kuṭiyēṟṟiṉar" eṉpatē, nām iṅku ārāytaṟkuriyatu. akattiyaṉār

   "vēntuviṉaiyiyaṟkai" eṉṟa tolkāppiya cūttirattiṉ avatārikaiyilum-"itu,
   malayamātavaṉ nilaṅkaṭanta neṭumuṭiyaṇṇaluḻai narapatiyaruṭaṉ koṇarnta
   patiṉeṇvakaik kuṭippiṟanta vēḷirkkum* vēntaṉ toḻil uritteṉkiṉṟatu"
   (poruḷati - 32) eṉa, maṟṟum orumuṟai eṭuttōti vaṟpuṟuttappaṭṭuḷatu.
   ippiṟkūṟṟilē, muṉkuṟitta toṭariṟkaṇṭa aruvāḷarai yoḻittu, oḻinta
   narapatiyaruṭaṉ kūṭiya vēḷirē kaṇṇaṉ vaḻiyiṉareṉavum, avar
   patiṉeṇvakaik kuṭiyiṉarāyiruntaṉar eṉavum,† avarellām aracurimai
   eytaṟkuriyavareṉavum kuṟikkap paṭutal kāṇalām. ivvāṟu, vēḷiraippaṟṟiya
   varalāṟṟai nacciṉārkkiṉiyar orumuṟaikku irumuṟai eḻututalāl, aḵtu etō
   oru piramāṇampaṟṟiyē avarkālattu vaḻaṅkiyiruttal vēṇṭu
   * srīviyāsapāratam, sapāparvam, 14-m atyāyattil, jarāsantaṉ viṣayamākak
       kaṇṇapirāṉ yutiṣṭararukkuk kūṟivarumiṭattu -- "rājāvē! jarāsantaṉ
       etirttalāṟ karaikaṭanta payam eṅkaṭku nērntapōtu, nāṅkaḷ patiṉeṇ
       kulattōrkaḷ cērntu ivvālōcaṉai ceytōm" eṉṟu, iru muṟai
       kuṟippiṭutalāl, yātavar patiṉeṇvakaik kuṭiyiṉarāyiruntamai
       teḷivākiṉṟatu. (srī ma.vī. irāmaṉujācāriyaravarkaḷ patippittuvarum
       viyāsapāratam tamiḻpoḻipeyarppup pārkka.) srīmat pākavatattil,
       ivarkaḷ aintu kulamākavum, 101-kiḷaikaḷākavum kūṟappaṭuvar.
       (tacamaskantam; at-1, 10)
       † muṉkuṟitta vākyattil, 'aracar patiṉeṇmaraiyum, patiṉeṇkuṭi
       vēḷiruḷḷiṭṭāraiyum' eṉa vēṟuvēṟākak kuṟikkappaṭṭiruppiṉum, iv
       vākyattil 'narapatiya ruṭaṉkoṇarnta 18-kuṭivēḷir' eṉa varuvataṉāl,

   "nīyē-vaṭapāṉ muṉivaṉ taṭaviṉuṭ ṭōṉṟic
       cempupuṉain tiyaṟṟiya cēṇeṭum puricai

       nāṟpattoṉpatu vaḻimuṟai vanta
       vēḷiruḷ vēḷē"

   eṉak kūṟiyirukkum aṭikaḷ puṟanāṉūṟṟil (201) kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
   itaṉporuḷ:- nītāṉ, vaṭatēcattu muṉivar oruvaratu yākattiṟṟōṉṟi, cempāl
   aḻakākac ceyyappaṭṭa mikapperiya kōṭṭaiyuṭaiya, veṟuppillāta
   poṉmayamākiya tuvārakaiyai āṇṭu, nāṟpattoṉpatu talaimuṟaiyākavanta
   vēḷirkkuḷḷē ciṟanta vēḷāy uḷḷaṉai-eṉpatām. ippaḻaiya mēṟkōḷāl,
   vēḷireṉpār tuvārakaiyiṉiṉṟuvantu teṉṉāṭāṇṭa ciṟṟaraca vakuppiṉar
   eṉpatumaṭṭil naṉku viḷaṅkiṟṟu; viḷaṅkavē, mēṟkūṟippōnta
   nacciṉārkkiṉiyar ceytikaḷilē, ciṟanta toṉṟaṟkup pirapala ātāraṅ
   kāṇappaṭṭatām.
   iṉi, vēḷir tuvārakaiyiṉiṉṟu vantēṟiyavar eṉpataṟku
   ilakkiyamuḷḷatāyiṉum, avar yātavakulattavareṉpatai viḷakkavalla
   piramāṇaṅkaḷai muṉṉūlkaḷiṉiṉṟu aṟital ippōtu aritām. āyiṉum, yatu
   vamicattōr ātiyiṟ kaṅkaipāyun tēcaṅkaḷiṟ palkip perukiyakālattē, aṉṉōr
   pakaivarkaḷāṟ* ṟuṉpamuṟātu vāḻtalvēṇṭi, akkulat talaivarākiya
   kaṇṇapirāṉ, mēlkaṭaṟpakkattē tuvārakaiyaip putitāka nirumittu, ataṉaic
   cūḻntu kiṭanta kāṭṭup piratēcaṅkaḷait tirutti nāṭukaḷākki eṇṇiṟanta
   yātavarkaḷai āṅkuk kuṭiyēṟṟit tām avarkaṭku irakṣakarāka niṉṟu utavi
   vantaṉar - eṉṟa ceyti purāṇētikācaṅkaḷiṟ kēṭkappaṭukiṉṟatu. kaṇṇapirāṉ

   eṉṟa pāṭṭāṟ kuṟittaṉar. ivvulakil aṟaṅkaḷ ceyyiṉ avai
   maṟumaikkutavu-meṉṟu maṟupayaṉ peṟum eṇṇattaṭaṉ tarumaviyāpāram ceyyum
   vāṇikalaṉṉaṉ āy; avaṉatu koṭai, periyōrkaḷ ceṉṟa vaḻiyeṉṟu ulakōr
   karutumpaṭi amaintatu" eṉpatu ippāṭṭiṉ pōnta poruḷ. iṉi, ammōciyār
   maṟṟōriṭattil,
   vaṭaticai yatuvē vāṉṟō yimayam
       teṉṟicai āay kuṭiyiṉ ṟāyiṟ
       piṟaḻvatu maṉṉōvim malartalai yulakē"

   eṉa ivvaḷḷal ulakiṟkupakārappaṭum ciṟappaip pukaḻvar. ivvaṭikaḷil
   amaikkappaṭṭa karuttu, potiyil malaikkuriya akattiyarkku vaḻaṅkum
   purāṇakkataiyaṭiyāka uṇṭākiyiruttal vēṇṭum.
   ivvāṟu, " āṭunaṭaip puraviyuṅ kaḷiṟun tērum - vāṭāyāṇar nāṭu mūrum -
   pāṭunark karukā āay aṇṭiraṉ" peruvaḷḷalāy ulakōr taṉṉai eṉṟum
   niṉaikkumpaṭi viḷaṅki, piṉ ' kālaṉeṉṉuṅ kaṇṇili yuyppa—mēlōrulaka

   muṭamōciyār, kuṭṭuvaṉkīraṉār eṉpōr. ivaruḷ, mōciyār, āyaṇṭiraṉ

   cuvarkkañ ceṉṟāṉ eṉpatām.
   iṉi, āy aṇṭiraṉaittavira, āy eyiṉaṉ eṉa maṟṟoruvaṉum akanāṉūṟṟiṟ pala

   nakaram uṟattūr eṉavum-- "yāḻicai maṟuki ṉīṭūr kiḻavōṉ - vāyvā ḷevvi

   cōṇāṭṭuc civastalaṅkaḷuḷ tirunīṭūr eṉpatoṉṟuṇṭu. miḻalaikkūṟṟattē
   miḻalai eṉap peyar vāynta taṉiyūr oṉṟuṇṭeṉpatum, atu civastala
   meṉpatum* " miḻalaināṭṭumiḻalaiyē veṇṇināṭṭumiḻalaiyē" eṉa varum srī-
   ;cuntaramūrttikaḷ tēvārappakutiyāl veḷiyām. (potu 1239-m pakkam)
   miḻalaikkūṟṟam allatu miḻalaināṭeṉpatu cōṇāṭṭiṉ kaṭaṟkaraip
   pakutiyāmpōtu, nīṭūr miḻalai eṉpavai, civastalaṅkaḷākiya tirunīṭūrum
   tiruvīḻimiḻalaiyumākak koḷḷal poruntuvatām.† im miḻalaikkūṟṟam
   pāṇṭināṭṭiṉ cila pakutiyaiyum muṉpu koṇṭiruntateṉpatu,
   tiruvālavāyuṭaiyār purāṇattil tirupperuntuṟaiyaic cārnta piratēcam
   miḻalaik kūṟṟattaic cārntiruntatākak kūṟappaṭṭiruttalāl veḷiyākiṉṟatu.‡
   iṉi evviyiṉ maṟṟōr ūrākiya uṟattūr nīṭāmaṅkalattai yaṭuttu uḷḷatu.
   orukāl, innīṭāmaṅkalamē nīṭūrāyiṉum ākalām.
   im miḻalaikkūṟṟattukkut talaivaṉākiya vēḷ evvi aruṅkuṇaṅkaḷum
   peruṅkoṭaiyum vāyntavaṉ eṉpatu puṟanāṉūṟṟil ivaṉaippaṟṟiyuḷḷa
   pāṭalkaḷāl viḷaṅkuvaṉavām; " ōmpā vīkai māvēḷ evvi" eṉa māṅkuṭikkiḻārum
   (puṟam-24) " irumpāṇokkaṟ ṟalaivaṉ" eṉa veḷḷerukkilaiyārum (ṣa)

   varuvaṉ eṉṟu colluvar' eṉṟu kūṟiṉāḷ; iḵtu eṉṉē! talaiva! nī avaṭku
   iraṅki aruḷ paṇṇātiruttal mikakkoṭiyatu" " mayilukkiraṅkip
   pōrvaiyaḷitta peruṅkaruṇaiyuṭaiya pēka! yām ippōtu pacittu niṉpāl
   varavillai; emmāṟ ṟāṅkappaṭavēṇṭiya cuṟṟamum ippōtu illai; pēraruḷāḷa!
   nī aṟaneṟiyil oḻuka vēṇṭum eṉpatē yām irakkum paricil: ātalāl nī uṉ
   tēril ēṟicceṉṟu, pārppataṟkariya tukkattaik koṇṭa uṉ kaṟpuṭai
   maṉaiviyiṉ cintākulattait tīrppāyāka" eṉṟaṉar. ivvāṟē, tām paricil
   vēṇṭi varavillai yeṉṟum, nī avaḷaicceṉṟu cērtalē yām vēṇṭuvateṉṟum--
   aricil kiḻārum, peruṅkuṉṟūr kiḻārum pēkaṉaikkaṇṭu pāṭiṉar. * iṅṅaṉam,
   akkālattē tamiḻ nāṭṭup perumakkaḷāy viḷaṅkiya pulavar vēṇṭukōḷukkup
   pēkaṉ iṉṉatu ceytāṉ eṉpatu terintilatēṉum, vēṇṭiyōrkku vēṇṭiyāṅkaḷikkum
   peruvaḷḷalākiya avaṉ, ittakaiya perumakkaḷ neṭuntūrattiliruntu vantu
   taṉpāl vēṇṭiya viruppattai niṟaivēṟṟātirāṉ eṉṟē nampalām.
   iṉi ivvēḷ - pēkaṉaippāṭiya aricilkiḻār, vēḷāvik kōmāṉ patumaṉukku
   makaḷvayiṟṟup peyaraṉākiya, takaṭūr eṟinta peruñcēralirum poṟaiyaip
   patiṟṟuppattu eṭṭāmpattāṟ pukaḻntuḷḷār. eṉavē, vēḷāvikkōviṉ
   vaḻiyiṉaṉākiya pēkaṉum, avvāvikkōviṉ makaḷ makaṉākiya peruñcēral
   irumpoṟaiyum orukālattavarātal peṟappaṭum. orukāl, pēkaṉ vēḷāvikkōmāṉ
   patumaṉukku makaṉākavēṉum aṉṟi makaṉ makaṉākavēṉum iruttalum kūṭum.
   vaiyāvikkōp perumpēkaṉ eṉṟu ivvaḷḷal vaḻaṅkappaṭutalāl, ivaṉūr
   vaiyāvipuriyākiya āvi naṉkuṭi eṉpatu terikiṟatu. ivvāvinaṉkuṭikkuc '
   cittaṉ vāḻvu' eṉpatum oru paḻampeyarām. itaṉai, " nallampar nalla
   kuṭiyuṭaittuc cittaṉ vāḻ - villan toṟu mūṉṟeriyuṭaittu - nallaravap -
   pāṭṭuṭaittuc cōmaṉ vaḻivanta pāṇṭiyaniṉ -
   * ippulavarkaḷ pāṭalkaḷaip puṟanāṉūṟṟil 143 - mutal 147 - varaiyuḷḷa
       pāṭalkaḷāl uṇarka.

   page 46
   nāṭṭuṭaittu nalla tamiḻ" eṉṉum auvaivākkāṉum aṟika*, ippāṭṭāṟ cittaṉ
   vāḻvākiya āvinaṉkuṭiyil, caṅkanāḷil antaṇar mikuntiruntamai veḷiyām.
   cittaṉ vāḻvu eṉpatu paḻaniyāṇṭavar, kōyil koṇṭamaipaṟṟi vanta
   peyar:"cittaṉ - piḷḷaiyārukkut tirunāmam" eṉpar nacciṉārkkiṉiyarum.

                               vēḷ - pāri.

   paḻaiya vēḷiruṭaiya varalāṟukaḷuḷḷē pāri eṉṉum peruntakaiyiṉ
   carittiram, iṉimaiyum perumaiyum irakkamumuṭaiyatākum. māriyaṉṉa
   varaiyā īkaiyāl ulakamuḷḷaḷavum ōṅku pukaḻ niṟuviya tamiḻ vaḷḷalkaḷil,
   ivvēḷirtalaivaṉaiyē mutal vaṉākac collal takum. tamiḻ mūvēntarum
   maṟṟaic ciṟṟaracarkaḷumpōla, ippāri, nāṭum poruḷum
   naṉkupaṭaittavaṉillaiyāyiṉum, ivaṉatu vaḷḷaṉmai avarellāraiyum perituṅ
   kīḻppaṭa vaittamaiyāl, ivaṉatu perumai malaimēl viḷakkuppōl
   viḷaṅkuvatāyiṟṟu. ivvēḷir talaivaṉatu vaḷḷaṉmaiyē, koṭaipperumaikku ōr
   ellaikkallākap piṟkālattup periyōrkaḷuṅ kuṟittuvaippārāyiṉar. ettaṉaiyō
   vaḷḷalkaḷ muṉṉāḷil viḷaṅkiyiruppiṉum, "koṭukkilātāṉaip pāriyē yeṉṟu
   kūṟiṉuṅ koṭuppārilai" eṉṉum arumaittiruvākkāṟ kuṟikkappaṭṭavaṉ,
   ipperuntakaiyēyaṉṟō. muṟkālattē pulavaraiyum iravalaraiyum ivaṉ evvāṟu
   pōṟṟivantava ṉeṉpatum, paṇṭaic centamiḻp pulavar ivaṉatu
   taṭaṅkaruṇaiyaiyum peruṅ koṭaiyaiyum eṅṅaṉam pukaḻntuḷḷāreṉpatum,
   piṟavaralāṟukaḷum caṅkanūlkaḷil ivvaḷḷalaip paṟṟiya pāṭalkaḷai
   nōkkuvārkku naṉku viḷaṅkum. annūlkaḷilum piṟavaṟṟilum ivaṉatu vāḻkkai
   paṟṟik kūṟum varalāṟukaḷai, cevvaṉam ārāyntu sētu
   *tirumurukāṟṟuppaṭaiyurai; pak - 27; nacciṉārkkiṉiyar, cittaṉ vāḻvu
       eṉpatu āvinaṉkuṭikku oru peyar eṉṟeḻutal kāṇka.

   samstāṉa vitvāṉ srī: rā. irākavaiyaṅkār avarkaḷ centamiḻp pattirikai

   cilavicēṭaṅkaḷaiyum eḻutuvēm.
   " pāri eṉpāṉ, tamiḻnāṭṭiṟ paṇṭaikkālattē perum pukaḻpeṟṟuviḷaṅkiya
   vaḷḷalkaḷ eḻuvaruḷ talaimai vāyntavaṉ. vēḷ eṉṉum paṭṭam peṟṟa
   uḻuvittuṇpōr vakaiyiṉaṉ. celvamikka munnūṟu ūrkaḷaiyuṭaiya

   valivuṭaiyatōr pēraraṇ amaittu ataṉait taṉ aracirukkaiyākki ataṉpāṟ
   ciṟakkavīṟṟiruntōṉ; ivaṉatumalaiyaraṇ, periya aḻakum ariya kāvalum
   uṭaiyatu(naṟṟiṇai.253) eṉavum, pakaivar muṟṟiyakālattum

   nellum palāppaḻamum vaḷḷikkiḻaṅkum naṟuntēṉum taṉpālmikkateṉavum,
   (puṟam-109) eṉṟum vaṟṟātatum pēriṉimai payappatumākiya kuḷirnta
   nīraiyuṭaiya paiñcuṉaiyoṉṟu taṉkaṇ uṭaiyateṉavum(akam-78;
   kuṟuntokai-166) cāṉṟōr kūṟuvar. ippaṟampu pāṇṭi nāṭṭateṉpatu, "
   varicait tamiḻpuṉai pāriyum pāṇṭiyaṉ maṇṭalamē" eṉṉum
   pāṇṭimaṇṭalaccatakac ceyyuḷāṟ (94) pulappaṭukiṟatu. ippāri, ' ulakuṭaṉ
   tiritarum palarpukaḻ nallicai – vāy moḻik kapilaṉ" (akam-78) eṉa
   nallicaip pulavarkaḷāl mēmpaṭuttēttappaṭṭa kapilar eṉṉum pulavar
   talaivarkku uyirt tōḻaṉāṉavaṉ. (puṟam-201.) " pulaṅ kantāka iravalar
   ceviṉē - varai purai kaḷiṟṟoṭu naṉkala ṉīyum - uraicāl vaṇpukaḻppāri"
   (akam- 303) eṉa auvaiyār pāṭutalāl, ivaṉ avarālum pēraṉpu

   pōla niṉṟu, taṉṉaiyaṭainta aṟiñar maṭavar valiyar meliyar yāvarkkum
   iṉṉaruḷ curantu, mūvēntariṉum

   mikutiyāka naṉku vaḻaṅkiya vaḷḷiyōṉ. ivaṉatu peruṅ koṭaikkuk kapilar

   cellumpōtu, mullaikkoṭiyoṉṟu paṭartaṟkuk koḻukompiṉṟi veṟṟiṭai

   "pūttalai yaṟāap puṉaikoṭi mullai

       paṭumaṇi yāṉaip paṟampiṟ kōmāṉ

       iravāma līnta iṟaivarpōl nīyuṅ
       karavāma līkai kaṭaṉ" (veṇpāmālai. pāṭāṇ. - 6)

   eṉpaṉavaṟṟāṟ pārāṭṭappaṭuvatu. taṉṉaracirukkaiyākiya paṟam,
   malaiyoḻiyat taṉṉāṭṭu munnūṟu ūrkaḷaiyum ivaṉ iravalarkkē aḷittaṉaṉ

   paricilar peṟṟaṉar" (puṟam -110) eṉpataṉāl uṇarka. ivaṉatu
   varaiyāvaṇmai "koṭukkilātāṉaip pāriyē yeṉṟu kūṟiṉuṅkoṭuppārilai"
   (tēvāram) eṉpataṉāl,

   page 49
   caivacamayācāriyarākiya cuntaramūrtti nāyaṉārālum eṭuttāḷappaṭṭatu
   eṉpatoṉṟē ivaṉatu vaḷḷaṟṟalaimaiyait teḷḷitiṟ pulappaṭuttum. iṅṅaṉam
   māripōṉṟa varaiyāvīkaiyāl, ivaṉ yāriṉum ciṟakkaviḷaitta perumpukaḻkku
   aḻukkāṟukoṇṭu, tamiḻ mūvēntarum oruṅkukūṭip paṭaiyeṭuttuppōy, ivaṉatu

   aṅkirunta kapilar eṉṉum pulavarperumāṉ kiḷikaḷai vaḷarttu viṭuttu

   pātukāttu vantaṉar.
   * aṭaikkappaṭṭa matiliṉuḷḷē taṅkutal

   ivvariyakatai--
   "ulakuṭaṉ tiritarum palarpukaḻ nallicai
       vāymoḻik kapilaṉ cūḻac cēyniṉṟu
       ceḻuñcen nelliṉ viḷaikatir koṇṭu
       neṭuntā ḷāmpaṉ malaroṭu kūṭṭi

       āḷiṭūuk kaṭantu vāḷama ruḻakki
       ēntukōṭ ṭiyāṉai vēnta rōṭṭiya
       kaṭumparip puravik kaivaṇ pāri
       tīmperum paiñcuṉaip pūtta
       tēṅkamaḻ putumalar nāṟumivaḷ nutalē"

   eṉavarum akappāṭṭālum (78) "itaṉuṭ 'kapilaṉ cūḻa' eṉṟatu - aracar
   mūvarum vaḷaittiruppa akappaṭṭiruntu, uṇavillāmaik kiḷikaḷai vaḷarttuk
   katirkoṇṭu varaviṭṭa katai" eṉa varum ataṉuraiyālum aṟiyappaṭum. ituvē-
   "uraicāl vaṇpukaḻp pāri paṟampi
       ṉiṟaipaṟaik kurīiyiṉaṅ kālaip pōki
       muṭaṅkupuṟac cennel tarīiyar ōrāṅku

   page 50
   iraitēr koṭpiṉa vākip poḻutupaṭap
       paṭarkoṇ mālaip paṭartan tāṅku" (akam-303)

   eṉpataṉāl auvaiyārālum eṭuttuk kūṟappaṭṭatām. piṉ kapilar, paṟampai
   muṟṟiya mūvēntaraiyum nōkki, "nīvir muttiṟattīrum oruṅkukūṭit
   tāṉaiyāṉai kutiraimutaliya paṭaikoṇṭu ettuṇaikkālam muṟṟip
   porutīrāyiṉum, ippāriyuṭaiya paṟampu ummāṟ koḷḷutal aritu; ivaṉatu
   munnūṟūraiyam ivaṉpāṟ pāṭippeṟṟa paricilarpōla nīvirum pāṭiṉarāyvariṉ,
   koḷḷutal eḷitu" eṉṟu, ivaṉatu puravalarkkarumaiyum iravalarkku
   eḷimaiyumākiya perunilaiyait tam iṉiyapāṭalāṉ aṟivikka, (puṟam-110)
   ataṉāl avvēntar ivaṉai etirttu vellutal ariteṉpatōrntu ivaṉōṭu
   porutaṟkañci ōṭiṉar eṉpar. mēlē, "ēntu kōṭṭi yāṉai vēnta rōṭṭiya –
   kaṭumparip puravik kaivaṇ pāri" eṉṟataṉāl, ivaṉ am mūvēntaraiyum
   veṉṟōṭṭiyamai naṉku pulappaṭum. itaṉpiṉ mūvēntarum oruṅkukūṭi vēṟoru
   cūḻcciceytu pāriyai vañcittuk koṉṟaṉar. itaṉai 112-m puṟappāṭṭuraiyil
   "oruvaṉai mūvēntarum muṟṟiyiruntu vañcittuk koṉṟamaiyiṉ" eṉavarutalāl
   aṟika. "pāriyum paricilar irappiṉ-vārēṉ eṉṉāṉ avarvarai yaṉṉē" (puṟam
   -108) eṉak kapilar pāriyiṉiyalpu kūṟutalāl, avvēntar mūvarum
   ivaṉiyalpukkut takap paricilar vēṭampūṇṭō, piṟaraip paricilarāka
   viṭuttō ivaṉai irantu tam -makappaṭuttik koṉṟaṉarātal vēṇṭum.
   ikkaruttu--
   "puricaip puṟattiṉiṟ cēraṉum cōḻaṉum* pōrpuriya
       iriyac cayaṅkoṇṭa pōtiṉil yāmiṉi yīṅkivaṉaip
       paricukku nalla kavipāṭi ṉālvarum pākyameṉṟē
       varicait tamiḻpuṉai pāriyum pāṇṭiyaṉ maṇṭalamē"

   eṉṉum pāṇṭimaṇṭala catakac ceyyuḷiṉum (64) payilvatu kaṇṭukoḷka.
   * innūluṭaiyār, pāṇṭimaṇṭalac ciṟappaiyē kūṟippōntavarātaliṉ,

   iṅṅaṉam, puravalarkku iṉṉāṉāy, iravalarkku iṉiyaṉāy viḷaṅkiya nallicai
   vaḷḷalāṉa vēḷ-pāri, mūvēntar cūḻcciyāl iṟanta piṉṉar, avaṉukku
   iṉṉuyirttōḻarākiya pulavar perumāṉāṉa kapilar, avvaḷḷalai nīttut
   tamittuyirvāḻtaṟku maṉamilarāyiṉum, avaṉuṭaiya aṟivuṭaimakaḷiraik
   kāppataṟku vēṟoruvarum ilarātalpaṟṟi uyirkoṇṭuniṉṟu, ammakaḷirkkut
   takka aṟivum perumaiyumuṭaiya kaṇavarait tēṭaniṉaintu, avarkaḷuṭaṉ, *
   avarkaṭkun tamakkum pēraṉpumikka paṟampiṉai viṭa muṭiyāmalē viṭuttu,
   appāriyai niṉaiyuntōṟum paṟampiṉait tirumpi nōkkuntōṟum uḷḷam
   nekkunekkurukik kaṇṇīr vāraniṉṟu, āṟṟoṇāt tuyarāṟ poṅkiyeḻunta aṉpuṭaip
   pāṭalkaḷāl pāriyaiyum paṟampu malaiyaiyum pukaḻntukoṇṭēceṉṟu
   ōrūriṟṟaṅkiṉar. aṅku aṉṟiravu nilāttōṉṟiyapōtu avaruṭaṉirunta pāriyiṉ
   arumaimakaḷir, tām itaṟku muntiya nilākkālattil tamatu aracunilaiyiṭṭa
   tiruvuṭainakarkkaṇṇē iṉitu makiḻntu viḷaiyāṭiyatum, aṭutta
   nilākkālattut tām tantai yiḻantu, taṇpaṟampiḻantu, tamiyarāyt
   †tuccilotuṅkit tuyar
   kūraniṉṟatum tammuḷḷattē tōṉṟa appōtu, maṉamuruki--
   "aṟṟait tiṅka ḷavveṇ ṇilaviṉ
       entaiyu muṭaiyōm, eṅkuṉṟum piṟarkoḷār;
       iṟṟait tiṅkaḷ ivveṇ ṇilaviṉ
       veṉṟeṟi muraciṉ vēntareṅ
       kuṉṟuṅ koṇṭār; yām entaiyu milamē." (puṟam-112)

   eṉṉum pāṭalaip pāṭiṉar. ippāṭalāl, icceyyuḷ ceytaṟku oru mātattukkumuṉ,
   pāri taṉṉaracirukkaiyākiya paṟampiṉ kaṇtaṉ makaḷir mutaliyōruṭaṉ iruntu
   vāḻntuvantavaṉeṉpatu pulappaṭum. ippāṭal caṅkattārāṟ ṟokukkappaṭṭa
   puṟanāṉūṟṟil oṉṟākak kāṇappaṭṭavāṟṟāl, immakaḷir nallicaip pulavarātal
   uṇarappaṭum
   * pāri iṟantapiṉ, avaṉ maṉaiviyaiyum kapilar aḻaittuc ceṉṟatāka
       iṉmaiyāl, avaḷ taṉ kaṇavaṉuṭaṉ uyirnīttaṉaḷ pōlum.
       † tuccil - piṟar iruppu

   page 52
   pārimakaḷir iruvar eṉpatu mēlē pulappaṭutaliṉ, īṇṭum 'pārimakaḷir
   pāṭiyatu' eṉap potuppaṭak kūṟappaṭṭamai paṟṟi, avviruvarumē
   centamiḻppulamaiyāṟ ciṟantavareṉpatu teḷivākum. pulavariruvar cērntu
   orupāṭal pāṭuvatu muṉṉai vaḻakkē. immakaḷiratu centamiḻppulamai,
   tammarumpeṟaṟ ṟantaiyākiya vaḷḷaṟpārikku āruyirttōḻarāṉa kapilar eṉṉum
   pulavar talaivarpāṟ peṟṟatām. itaṉmēṟ kapilar avvūrai viṭuttu
   iḷaviccikkō eṉpavaṉiṭañceṉṟu, immakaḷiratu uyar kuṭippiṟappu
   mutaliyavaṟṟai eṭutturaittu ivarkaḷai maṇañ ceytu koḷḷumpaṭi avaṉaip
   pāṭi vēṇṭa avaṉuṭampaṭāmaiyāl, iruṅkōvēḷ eṉpāṉiṭañ ceṉṟu avaṉaiyum
   avvāṟu vēṇṭa avaṉum aṅṅaṉamē uṭampaṭāṉāy maṟukka, itaṟkāka avaṉai
   muṉintupāṭi, pārikuṭikkum mūvēntarkkum uṇṭākiya pakaimaipaṟṟi
   aracaroruvarum ivarkaḷai maṇañceytu koḷḷa iyaiyāmaiyāl maṉanontu,
   kapilar, ammakaḷirait tamakkiṉiya pārppār cilarpāṟpaṭuttu, pāriyiṉ
   pirivāṟṟātu vaṭakkiruppārāyiṉar. itaṟkiṭaiyil, kapilar
   celvakkaṭuṅkōvāḻiyātaṉ eṉṟa cēramāṉpāṟ ceṉṟu, avaṉaip pattuppāṭalkaḷāṟ
   pukaḻntupāṭi nūṟāyiraṅkāṇamum avaṉ malaimēlēṟik kaṇṭukoṭutta nāṭum
   avaṉpāṟ peṟṟaṉar eṉat terikiṉṟatu. ituvē patiṟṟuppattiṉuḷ ēḻām
   pattāvatu. ivar, pāri iṟantapiṉṉētāṉ, avaṉatu naṟkuṇa naṟceyalkaḷ
   celvakkaṭuṅkō vāḻiyātaṉiṭamum iruppaṉavākak kēṭṭu avaṉaik
   kāṇacceṉṟaṉarāvar. itaṉai-
   "palā am paḻutta pacumpuṇ ṇariyal
       vāṭai tūkkum nāṭukeḻu peruviṟal
       ōvat taṉṉa viṉaipuṉai nalliṟ
       pāvai yaṉṉa nallōḷ kaṇavaṉ
       poṉṉi ṉaṉṉa pūviṟ paciyilaip
       puṉkā luṉṉattut talaiva ṉeṅkōp
       pularnta cāntiṟ pularā vīkai
       malarnta mārpiṉ mārivaṇ pāri

   page 53
   muḻavumaṇ pularā iravala riṉaiya
       vārāc cēṭpulam paṭarntō ṉaḷikkeṉa
       irakku vārēṉ eñcik kūṟēṉ
       ītta tiraṅkāṉ īttoṟu makiḻāṉ
       amarā vaḷḷiyaṉ eṉṉa nuvaluniṉ
       nallicai taravan ticiṉē"*

   eṉṉum patiṟṟuppattāṉ (7-m pattu. 1) aṟika. iccēraṉpāl ivar ceṉṟakālattu
   ippārimakaḷirum uṭaṉiruntaṉarpōlum. immakaḷir varalāṟṟiṉaip puṟanāṉūṟu
   oṉṟē tuṇaiyākakkoṇṭu ārāyiṉ, ataṉkaṇ--
   112-m pāṭṭu:- itu pāri makaḷir pāṭiyatu.
   113:- iḵtu avaṉ (pāri) makaḷiraip pārppārp paṭukkakkoṇṭu pōvāṉ
   paṟampuviṭutta kapilar pāṭiyatu.
   114-:- avaṉ makaḷiraikkoṇṭupōm kapilar paṟampu nōkkiniṉṟu colliyatu.
   200:-itu pāri makaḷirai viccikkōṉuḻaikkoṇṭu ceṉṟa kapilar pāṭiyatu.
   201:-itu pāri makaḷirai iruṅkō vēḷuḻaik koṇṭu ceṉṟa kapilar pāṭiyatu.
   202:-iruṅkōvēḷ pārimakaḷiraik koḷḷāṉākak kapilar pāṭiyatu.
   236:-vēḷ-pāri tuñciyavaḻi, makaḷiraip pārppārp paṭuttu vaṭakkirunta
   kapilar pāṭiyatu.
   * "paṭarntōṉ eṉpatu muṟṟu; aḷikkeṉa eṉpatu nī emmai aḷippāyāka
       eṉaccolli eṉṟavāṟu; irakku eṉpatu taṉviṉai. eñcikkūṟēṉ eṉpatu,
       uṇmaiyiṉ ellaiyaikkaṭantu poyyē pukaḻntu collēṉ e-ṟu. yāṉ, pāri
       cēṭpulam paṭarntāṉ: nī emmai aḷikka: eṉac colli, irakkaveṉṟuvantu
       cila pukaḻntu colkiṉṟēṉum allēṉ; aḵtaṉṟi yāṉ uṇmaiyoḻiyap pukaḻntu
       colkiṉṟēṉum allēṉ; īttatiraṅkāmai mutalākiya pāri naṟkuṇaṅkaḷai
       niṉpāl uḷavāka ulakañ collum niṉpukaḻ niṉpālē taravantēṉ"- eṉpatu
       ivvaṭikaḷiṉ paḻaiya urai.

   eṉa varuvaṉavaṟṟāl, pārimakaḷir oru cilar eṉpatum, avar pāṭa
   vallavareṉpatum, appāri iṟantapiṉ avaṉatu tōḻarākiya kapilareṉṉum
   pulavarantaṇarāl iḷaviccikkō iruṅkōvēḷ eṉṉum aracariṭam tammai
   maṇañceytu koḷḷumpaṭi vēṇṭap paṭṭaṉareṉpatum, avvaracar ataṟku
   uṭampaṭāmaiyāṟ kapilarāṟ pārppār paṭukkappaṭṭaṉa reṉpatum.* ivaraip
   pārppārp paṭuttapiṉ kapilar pirivāṟṟātu vaṭakkiruntaṉa reṉpatum
   aṟiyappaṭum
   * kapilar pārimakaḷiraip pārppārp paṭuttaṉar eṉpatu, am makaḷirai
       aracaroruvarum maṇañceytu koḷḷāmaiyāl., avarait tamakkiṉiya pārppār
       cilaratu pātukāvalil vaittamaiyē kuṟikkumeṉap pala niyāyaṅkāṭṭi
       eḻutuvar- srī rā.irākavaiyaṅkār avarkaḷ.

   iṅṅaṉam, pārimakaḷirai aracarevarum maṇam purintu koḷḷāmaiyāl,
   avarkaḷait tamakku vēṇṭiya pārppārcilaratu pātukāvalil vaittut
   tammuyirkkuyirāyirunta vaḷḷaliṟantataṉpiṉ ulakattē tām uyirvāḻa
   oruppaṭātu--" pāri! nīyum yāṉuṅkalanta naṭpiṟkupporunta yāṉum niṉṉuṭaṉ
   kūṭappōvataṟku maṉamiyaiyāmaiyāl 'nī īṇṭut tavirka' eṉṟu colli—ivvāṟu
   māṟupaṭṭaṉaiyātaliṉ, niṉakku nāṉ poruntiṉava ṉallēṉ; ataṉāl, nīeṉakku
   utaviceyta kālaṅkaḷilum eṉṉai veṟuttiruntaṉaipōlum; iṅṅaṉamāyiṉum,
   ippiṟappiṉkaṇ nīyum nāṉuṅkūṭi iṉpuṟṟiruntavāṟupōla, maṟupiṟappiṉum
   niṉṉoṭukūṭi vāḻtalai viti kūṭṭuvatāka" eṉṟa karuttuppaṭa—
   "malaikeḻu nāṭa māvaṇ pāri!
       kalantakēṇmaikkovvāy,nīyeṟ
       pulantaṉai yākuvai puranta yāṇṭē;
       peruntaku ciṟappi ṉaṭpiṟ kollātu
       oruṅku varalviṭā toḻikeṉak kūṟi
       iṉaiyai yātali, ṉiṉakku maṟṟiyāṉ
       mēviṉē ṉaṉmaiyāṉē; āyiṉum
       immaipōlakkāṭṭi, ummai

   page 55
   yiṭaiyil kāṭci niṉṉōṭu
       uṭaṉuṟai vākkuka; uyarnta pālē"

   eṉṟa pāṭalāl uḷḷamuruki, pirayōpavēcañ ceytaṟku vaṭakkiruntu* uyir
   nīttaṉar. ippāṭṭil, "naṭpiṟ kollātu -oruṅku varalviṭā toḻikeṉak kūṟi"
   eṉa varutalāl, pāriyuṭaṉ tāmum iṟakkat tuṇintirunta kapilarai avvaḷḷal
   taṭuttavaṉ eṉpatu uṇarappaṭum. iṅṅaṉam kapilarait taṭuttatu, pāri,
   taṉmakaḷirai ippulavaraik koṇṭu vāḻkkaippaṭuttaṟkuppōlum.
   * vaṭakkiruttal eṉpatu, kiḻakkumukamākat taruppaikaḷaipparappi vaṭakku
       nōkkiyiruntu, tammuyirviṭac ceytukoḷḷum oru mahāviratamām. caṅka
       kālattil ivvaḻakku mikutiyāka vaḻaṅkiyateṉpatu, paḻaiyanūlkaḷāl
       naṉkaṟiyappaṭukiṉṟatu. itaṉaip 'pirāyōpavēcam' eṉpar vaṭanūlār.

   ivvaḷavē, puṟanāṉūṟu mutaliya paḻaiya nūlkaḷiṟ pāriyiṉ varalāṟākak
   kāṇappaṭuvaṉa. āyiṉum, piṟkālattavarāl tokukkap paṭṭatum palacarittira
   vicēṭaṅkaḷaik koṇṭatumāṉa tamiḻ nāvalar caritai eṉṉum nūliṉiṉṟu,
   pārimakaḷir pārppārp paṭukkap paṭṭapiṉ nikaḻnta vēṟucila ceytikaḷaiyum
   "auvaiyār" eṉṟa viyācattē, sētucamstāṉa vitvāṉ srī rā. irākavaiyaṅkār
   avarkaḷ eḻutiyirukkiṟārkaḷ. avaiyāvaṉa:- kapilar, aracar oruvarum
   iyaiyāmaiyāṟ pārimakaḷirai maṇampurintu koṭukkaviyalāmal, avarait
   tamakku vēṇṭiya pārppār cilaratu pātukāvalil vaittuviṭṭut tām
   vaṭakkiruntu uyir nīttaṉar - eṉṟa ceyti, auvaiyār eṉṉum aruntamiḻ
   mūtāṭṭikkut teriyavara, avar mēṉikaḻntavaṟṟukku maṉanontu, appāri
   makaḷir irunta tirukkōvalūrkkuc ceṉṟu, ammakaḷiraikkaṇṭu, avarkku
   nērnta peruntuyarkku mikavum varunti, maḻaiyāl naṉaintu vanta tamakku
   avarkaḷ oru nīlacciṟṟāṭai aḷikka, appōtu--
   ----------------
   page 56
   "pāri paṟitta paṟiyum* paḻaiyaṉūrk
       kāriyaṉ ṟīnta kaḷaikkōlum† --cēramāṉ
       vārāyō eṉṟaḻaitta vārttaiyum‡ immūṉṟum
       nīlacciṟ ṟāṭaikku nēr" (tamiḻnāvalar caritai)

   eṉṟa aḻakiya pāṭalaippāṭi, aṉṟiravu avarkaḷ tamakkiṭṭa ilaikkaṟiyamutai

       kaṭakañ ceṟiyātō kaikku" (௸)

   * auvaiyār pāripāṟ paricil peṟac ceṉṟapōtu, avvaḷḷal ivaḷarumai

       ivar avaṉaip paricilīntu viṭukkumpaṭi vaṟpuṟutta, avvēṇṭutalai avaṉ
       maṟuttaṟkiyalāṉāy vēṇṭiyavaṟṟai aḷittu viṭuttum, ivar pirivāṟṟātu
       taṉpāl ivar tirumpivarac cūḻcciceytu, ivarcellum vaḻiyiṟ cilaraip
       pōkkit taṉpāṟpeṟṟa poruḷkaḷaip paṟittuvarumpaṭiceyya, ataṉāl peṟṟa
       paricilkaḷaiyiḻanta auvaiyār, pāriyeṇṇiyapaṭiyē mīṇṭuvantu
       avvaḷḷaliṭam nikaḻntatai aṟivikka, avaṉ mukattāl nontu, avar
       mīṇṭamaikku akattāl uvantu neṭuṅkālam aḷavaḷāvip perumakiḻveyti,
       muṉṉilum mikutiyāka arumporuḷ nalki viṭuttaṉaṉ" eṉavum;

       aṅṅaṉamē aḷantu aṉṟu viṭaipeṟakkūṭāmaiyāl piṉṉum avaṉpāṟ cilakālan
       taṅkiṉār eṉavum,
       ‡ cēraṉ araṇmaṉaiyil, viruntiṟkuvanta palaruṭaṉ auvaiyārum uṇṭaṟku

       avaṉuṇṇattakka iṭam oḻikka niṉaintu, āṇṭuḷḷa palarum vēṟṟōrātaliṉ

   page 57
   eṉṟa veṇpāvaik kūṟi, avarkaḷai naṉṉilaiyil niṟuttuṅ kavalaiyē
   perituṭaiyarāy, āṇṭirunta teyvīkaṉ eṉṉum aracaṉoruvaṉai, ivarkaḷai
   maṇam puriyumpaṭi vēṇṭi uṭampaṭuvittaṉar. avvaracaṉ uṭampaṭṭatum,
   ittirumaṇattiṟkut tāmpeṟṟa teyvattaṉmaiyāl vēṇṭuvaṉa aṉaittum uṇṭākki,
   mūvēntarkkum pārikuṭikkumuḷḷa pakaimaiyum pōkki, ammūṉṟaracaraiyum tam
   aṟiviṉ valimaiyāṟ kōvalūrkku varavaḻaittu appāri makaḷiratu
   perumaṇattaic ciṟappa iyaṟṟiṉar. ivvāṟu, pāri makaḷir maṇam auvaiyārāṟ
   ciṟakka nikaḻttappaṭṭamai aṟiyappaṭutalāl, ' kapilar pārimakaḷiraip
   pārppārp paṭuttaṉa' reṉpatu, avar ammakaḷiraip pārppāratu pātukāppil
   vaittamaiyaiyē kuṟikkum. ipperumaṇattil auvaiyār, paṉantuṇṭam
   paḻantaravum, peṇṇaiyāṟu neypāl talaippeytu varavum, vāṉam poṉmāri
   poḻiyavum pāṭit tamatu teyvavākkiṉ valimai uṇarttiṉar eṉpar. peṇṇaiyāṟu
   ivar pāṭalukku neypāl koṇarntu vanta katai, villiputtūrar makaṉār
   varantaruvārālum--
   " auvaipā ṭalukku naṟuneypāl peruki
       yaruntami ḻaṟiviṉāṟ ciṟantu
       * * *
       teyvamā natinīr parakkunā ṭantat
       tirumuṉaip pāṭinaṉ ṉāṭu"

   eṉa eṭuttāḷappaṭṭamai kāṇka. mēṟkūṟiyavaṟṟai yellām tamiḻ nāvalar
   caritaiyil---
   " auvaiyār aṅkavai caṅkavaiyait teyvīkaṉukkuk kalyāṇam
       paṇṇuvikkiṟapōtu ōlaiyeḻuta vināyakaṉai aḻaitta veṇpā.
       orukai yirumaruppu mummatattu nālvāyk
       kariyuruvak kaṅkāḷaṉ cemmal--karimukavaṉ
       kaṇṇāla vōlai kaṭiteḻuta vārāṉēṟ
       ṟaṉṉāṇmai tīrppaṉ capittu.

   -------------
   page 58
   cēra cōḻa pāṇṭiyarkku vināyakaṉeḻutap pāṭiya veṇpākkaḷ.
   cēralarkōṉ cēral ceḻumpūn tirukkōva
       lūraḷavun tāṉvaruka uṭkātē -- pārimakaḷ
       aṅkavaiyaik koḷḷa aracaṉ maṉamiyaintāṉ
       caṅkiyā tēvaruka tāṉ.
       pukārmaṉṉaṉ poṉṉit tirunāṭaṉ cōḻaṉ
       takāteṉṟu tāṉaṅ kiruntu - nakātē
       kaṭuka varuka kaṭikkōva lūrkku
       viṭiyal patiṉeṭṭā nāḷ.
       vaiyat tuṟaivaṉ maturā puritteṉṉaṉ
       ceyyat takāteṉṟu tēmpātē - taiyaṟku
       vēṇṭuvaṉa koṇṭu viṭiyalpati ṉeṭṭānāḷ
       īṇṭu varuka iyaintu.

   mūvarum vantapōtu paṉantuṇṭattaip pāṭiyatu.
   tiṅkaṭ kuṭaiyuṭaic cēraṉuñ cōḻaṉum pāṇṭiyaṉum
       maṅkaik kaṟukiṭa *vantuniṉ ṟārmaṇap pantarilē
       caṅkokka veṇkurut tīṉṟupac cōlai calacalattuk
       koṅkiṟ kuṟatti kuvimulai pōlak kurumpaiviṭṭu
       nuṅkukkaṇ muṟṟi yaṭikkaṇ kaṟuttu nuṉicivantu
       paṅkukku mūṉṟu paḻantara vēṇṭum paṉantuṇṭamē.

   antak kalyāṇattil, peṇṇaiyāṟṟaip pāṭiyatu.
   mutteṟiyum peṇṇai mutunī ratutavirntu
       tattivaru neypāl talaippeytu - kuttic
       cerumalaittey vīkaṉ tirukkōva lūrkku
       varumaḷaviṟ koṇṭāṭi vā.
       * aṟukiṭutal -- orumaṇaviṉai.

   --------------
   page 59
   appōtu varuṇaṉaip pāṭiyatu.
   "karuṇaiyā lintak kaṭalulakaṅ kākkum
       varuṇaṉē māmalaiyaṉ kōvaṟ -- perumaṇattu
       naṉmāri tāḻkkoṇṭa naṉṉī ratutavirttup
       poṉmāri yākap poḻi."

   eṉa varuvaṉavaṟṟāl aṟiyalām. ivaṟṟāl, aṅkavai caṅkavai yeṉpār
   pārimakaḷir eṉpatum, ammakaḷir tirukkōvalūril teyvīka ṉeṉṉum aracaṉukku
   maṇañceyyappaṭṭaṉareṉpatum, immaṇam auvaiyāratu aṟiviṉ māṭciyālum
   teyvattaṉmaiyālum ciṟappa nikaḻttappaṭṭa teṉpatum, auvaiyār
   mūvēntaraiyum im maṇattiṟku 'uṭkātu' 'nakātu' 'ceyyat takāteṉṟu
   tēmpātu' varuka eṉṟaḻaittamaiyāl, pārikuṭikkum avvēntarkkum uṇṭākiya
   paḻaiya ceṟṟampōkki varuka eṉṟaṉa reṉpatum, piṟavum ārāyntaṟiyappaṭum.

   uyttuṇarap paṭukiṟatu.

   eṉpatai avar varalāṟu kūṟumiṭattu viḷakkiyuḷḷēṉ*. āṉāl,

   vakuppiṉarākiya kaṭampar †marapeṉṟal poruttamākat terikiṟatu. āti
   carittirattai ārāyvōmāyiṉ, caḷukkaruṅ kaṭamparum orukulattavareṉpatu
   naṉku viḷaṅkum; tammaip piṟappitta muṉivariṭattiliruntu peṟṟa "
   ārītiputtirar" eṉṟa kulappeyarum, māṉavya kōttiramum murukakkaṭavuḷait
   taṅkula teyvamākak koṇṭamaiyum caḷukkarkkum kaṭamparkkum
   vittiyācamiṉṟiyē kāṇappaṭuvaṉavām. ivaruṭ kaṭampar, tammirukkaikkup
   pakkattē ( taṅkulateyvamākiya murukakkaṭavuṭkuriyatu paṟṟip pōlum.)
   kaṭampa maramoṉṟai vaḷarttu ataṉaip peritum pōṟṟi vantataṉāl appeyar
   peṟalāyiṉar. ‡ ivvāṟu kaṭampamarattai vaḷarttup pōṟṟivanta
   vēḷirvakuppār, caṅkakālattil, innaṉṉaṉ vaḻiyiṉarākavē
   kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉar. patiṟṟuppattiṉ nāṉkāmpattup patikattil " uruḷpūṅ
   kaṭampiṉ peruvāyil naṉṉaṉ" eṉṟu kūṟappaṭutal kāṇka. itaṉ poruḷ:--
   uruṭciyākiya pūvaiyuṭaiya kaṭampa marattait taṉ periya vāyiṟkaṇṇē koṇṭa
   naṉṉaṉ" eṉpatām. patiṟṟuppatturaikārar 'kaṭampiṉ peruvāyil - naṉṉaṉ ūr'
   eṉpar; ituvum kaṭampamarattai vāyiliṟkoṇṭa kāraṇam paṟṟiyē
   vaḻaṅkiyatākal uyttuṇarappaṭum. naṉṉaṉukku vākai kāvaṉ marameṉṟu
   appatikattil vēṟu kūṟappaṭutalāl ( pak - 58.) ikkaṭampu akkulattiṉar
   koṇṭa aṭaiyāḷamaramākum. ivaṟṟāl, naṉṉaṉ vēḷiruḷḷē kaṭampar
   * innūliṉ muṟpakutiyākiya vēḷirvaralāṟu 14-m pakkaṅkāṇka.

   pirivaiccērntava ṉeṉpatu uyttaṟiyalām.* iṉi, ikkaṭamparkkum vēḷir eṉṟa
   colvaḻakkam muṉpu vaḻaṅkiyateṉpataṟku, nīṇṭakālam ivarāṇṭa
   piratēcamoṉṟu ivar peyarāl " vēḷ kirāmam" eṉa
   vaḻaṅkiyatē cāṉṟākum. ippiratēcam ippōtu " pēlkām" (Belgaum) eṉat
   tirintu, pampāy rājatāṉiyil oru 'jillā'vāka vuḷḷamai muṉṉarē
   pirastāpikkap-paṭṭatu.†
   iṉi, vēḷirākiya caḷukkiyar makatanāṭṭu āntirariṉiṉṟu pirintavar
   eṉpataṟkuriya kāraṇaṅkaḷ vēḷir varalāṟṟuḷ viḷakkap paṭṭaṉa. eṉavē,
   accaḷukkaratu cakōtara vakuppiṉarākiya kaṭampavēḷirum
   avvāntiravamicattavarē eṉpatu collātē peṟappaṭum. iṟṟaikku 1700-
   varuṣaṅkaṭku muṉpu, kaṭampar marapaic cērnta ārītiputracātakarṇi eṉṟa
   vēntaṉoruvaṉ takṣiṇattai āṇṭaṉaṉ eṉpatu paḻaiya cāsaṉamoṉṟāl
   terikiṟatu; avvaracaṉ peyaruḷ muṟpakuti avaṉatu iyaṟpeyareṉṟum,
   piṟpakutiyākiya cātakarṇi eṉpatu, avaṉ kulappeyarā meṉṟum ākavē,
   akkaṭampavēntaṉ āntirarōṭu toṭarpuṭaiyavaṉātal vēṇṭumeṉṟum takka
   kāraṇaṅkaḷuṭaṉ carittiranūlōr kūṟuvar.‡ ( cātakarṇi- āntirapiruttiya
   aracar cilarkku vaḻaṅkiya peyar.) eṉavē, caḷukkiyaraippōlavē,
   kaṭamparum ātiyil āntirariṉiṉṟu pirinta vakuppāreṉpatu valipeṟuvatu
   kaṇṭukoḷka.
   naṉṉaṉ kulattavar, mēṟkuṟittavāṟu āntira vamicattavarē eṉpataṟku, vēṟu
   cila cāṉṟukaḷum kāṭṭalām. naṉṉaṉvaḻiyil vanta iḷaiñaṉoruvaṉ
   iḷavaccirakkō eṉṉum peyaruṭaiyāṉ eṉpatu puṟanāṉūṟṟāl aṟiyappaṭum.
   iḷavaccirakkō eṉpatu iḷaviccikkō eṉat tirittum vaḻaṅkappaṭukiṟatu.

   peyarkoṇṭu ivaṉ muṉṉōr vaccirakkō, allatu viccikkō
   eṉṟaḻaikkappaṭṭaṉar-- eṉpatu peṟalākiṉṟatu. ivvāṟē,
   naṉṉaṉvaḻiyiṉaṉoruvaṉ "vilkeḻu tāṉai vicciyar perumakaṉ" eṉak
   kuṟuntokaiyiṟ (328) kūṟappaṭutaluṅ kāṇka. vaccirakkō eṉpataṟku
   vacciranāṭṭaracaṉ eṉpatu poruḷām. vaccira nāṭu eṉpatoṉṟu tamiḻakattē
   muṟkālattu vaḻaṅkiyatāka ippōtu terintilatu; ātaliṉ, ippeyar,
   naṉṉaṉvaḻiyiṉarkku avar ātitēcattaippaṟṟiyē vaḻaṅkiyatākal vēṇṭum. "
   mānīr vēli vaccira naṉṉāṭṭukkōṉ" eṉṉuñ cilappatikāra aṭiyiṉ
   vicēṭavuraiyilē, aṭiyārkku nallār, vacciranāṭu – cōṇainatik
   karaiyiluḷḷa (Son)* tēyameṉak kuṟippiṭukiṉṟaṉar. (pak-139) aḵtāvatu--
   āntiracakkaravarttikaḷ āṭcipurinta makata tēcattiṉ teṉpakutiyām.
   ikkālaic carittiranūlkaḷil, vacciratēcameṉa oṉṟu taṉittu
   vaḻaṅkappaṭṭilatēṉum, muṟkālattē atu makata nāṭṭiṉ pirivukaḷil oṉṟāka
   aṭaṅkiyiruntateṉṟu terikiṉṟatu. ivvāṟu, naṉṉaṉ kulattavar āntirar
   eṉpataṟku muṉpu kūṟivanta kāraṇaṅkaḷōṭu porunta, avvāntirarkkuriya
   vacciranāṭṭai innaṉṉaṉ vaḻiyiṉarkkum vaḻaṅkiyiruttal iṅkē cintikkat
   takkatu. naṉṉaṉatu palkuṉṟak kōṭṭattukkē ' vacciranāṭu' allatu
   'viccināṭu' eṉṉum peyaru muṇṭeṉṟu piṉṉārāycciyāl viḷaṅkiṉum, atuvum,
   avaṉmuṉṉōratu kulatēcampaṟṟiyē vaḻaṅkiyatākal vēṇṭumeṉṟu
   karutattakkatu. aṉṟiyum, naṉṉaṉ nāṭākiya naṭunāṭṭukku makatanāṭu eṉṟa
   peyar muṉṉāḷil vaḻaṅki vantateṉpatu cāsaṉaṅkaḷālum pāṭalkaḷālum
   naṉkaṟintatu.† ituvum naṉṉaṉkulattōr tam pūrvatēcattai apimāṉittu
   vaḻaṅkiyatēyām. curuṅkac collumiṭattu, "vēḷir" eṉṟu tamiḻ nūlkaḷiṟ
   kūṟappaṭṭōr, paḻaiyakālattē, makata nāṭāṇṭa āntirariṉiṉṟu pirintuvantu
   teṉṉāṭāṇṭa ciṟṟaracar eṉpatē muṭipeṉṉumpaṭi paḻaiya nūlkaḷum piṟavuṅ
   kuṟippikkiṉṟaṉa
   eṉalām.

   vēḷirkulattē caṅkakālattil tamiḻakampukaḻa viḷaṅkiya peru vaḷḷalkaḷ
   mēṟkūṟippōnta vēḷir aṟuvarumēyāvar. ivarkaḷaṉṟi, tamiḻ vēntarākiya
   cēracōḻapāṇṭiyar cārpiṟ ciṟṟaracarākavum, makaṭkoṭuttuc campantañ
   ceytavarākavum, amaiccarākavum, paṭaittalaimai,tantirat talaimai
   vakittavarākavum amarnta vēḷkulat talaivar pallōreṉṟu terikiṉṟatu.
   nāṅkūrvēḷ aḻuntūrvēḷ eṉpōr, cōḻaṉ-uruvappaḵṟēr iḷañcēṭ ceṉṉikkum avaṉ
   makaṉ karikāṟperuvaḷattāṉukkum muṟaiyē peṇkoṭuttavarkaḷ. (tol.poruḷati.
   pak.54) maiyūr kiḻāṉākiya vēṇmāṉ, iḷañcēralirumpoṟaiyiṉ tāyppāṭṭaṉum
   amaiccaṉumāka viḷaṅkiyavaṉ. (patiṟṟup.-9-pattu) naṉṉaṉ vēṇmāṉ
   aḻumpilvēḷ eṉpōr, cēraratu paṭait talaimaiyum tantirattalaimaiyum
   muṟaiyē vakittavar. iṉi, ivvēḷirkaḷ patiṉālvar (135) eṉavum,
   patiṉoruvareṉavum (246) akanāṉūṟṟuṭ kūṟappaṭukiṉṟaṉar. ivariṟ
   patiṉālvar cērntu kāmūrttalaivaṉāṉa kaḻuvuḷ eṉṉum iṭaikkulattuc
   ciṟṟaracaṉaip* pōril veṉṟaṉar eṉpatum, karikāl peruvaḷattāṉ veṇṇivāyil
   eṉṉumiṭattu nikaḻttiya perum pōrilē tōṟṟavaruḷ patiṉoruvēḷir cērntavar
   eṉpatum 135, 246-m akappāṭṭukkaḷ kuṟikkiṉṟaṉa. vēḷirkkum titiyaṉ
   eṉpāṉukkum nikaḻnta pōroṉṟum annūliṟ kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu.(332) iṉic caṅka
   nāḷil viḷaṅkiya vēṟu vēḷkulattavarākat terintōr--veḷiyaṉ vēṇmāṉ,
   piṭavūr vēṇmāṉ, neṭuvēḷātaṉ, iḷaviccikkō, iruṅkōvēḷ, ālañcēri-ayintaṉ
   mutaliyōrāvar. ivarkaḷaip paṟṟiya varalāṟukaḷ teḷivāka viḷaṅka
   iṭamillai:ātaliṉ nūlkaḷ nōkki uṇarumāṟu viṭappaṭṭaṉa.
   ivvāṟu paḻaiya vēḷir varalāṟukaḷ,caṅkanūlkaḷiṟ kūṟap paṭṭavāṟu
   ārāyappaṭṭaṉa. ivar vaḻiyiṉarāyp piṟkālattu
   *patiṟṟuppattu,71,88-m pāṭalkaḷilum ivaṉceyti varukiṉṟatu.

   viḷaṅkiyavar, vēḷār eṉṉum peyaruṭaṉ cōḻar mutaliya aracarālum palavāṟu
